PROBLEM
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 14 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You would like to visit Florence for 6 days. You want to meet a friend in Florence between day 9 and day 14. You would like to visit Barcelona for 5 days. You would like to visit Helsinki for 5 days.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Barcelona and Florence, Helsinki and Barcelona.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 14 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 14 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You would like to visit Florence for 6 days. You want to meet a friend in Florence between day 9 and day 14. You would like to visit Barcelona for 5 days. You would like to visit Helsinki for 5 days.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Barcelona and Florence, Helsinki and Barcelona.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 14 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain travel_domain)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types city day amount)

  (:predicates
    (at_city ?c - city)
    (current_day ?d - day)
    (visit_count ?c - city ?a - amount)
    (can_fly ?c1 - city ?c2 - city)
    (next_day ?d1 - day ?d2 - day)
    (next_amount ?a1 - amount ?a2 - amount)
    (is_friend_window ?d - day)
    (met_friend)
  )

  ;; The agent starts the trip by arriving at the first city on Day 1.
  (:action arrive
    :parameters (?to - city ?d_start - day ?d_first - day ?c_zero - amount ?c_one - amount)
    :precondition (and 
      (at_city origin) 
      (current_day ?d_start) 
      (next_day ?d_start ?d_first)
      (visit_count ?to ?c_zero) 
      (next_amount ?c_zero ?c_one)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (at_city origin)) 
      (at_city ?to)
      (not (current_day ?d_start)) 
      (current_day ?d_first)
      (not (visit_count ?to ?c_zero)) 
      (visit_count ?to ?c_one)
    )
  )

  ;; The agent spends a full day in their current city.
  (:action stay
    :parameters (?c - city ?d_prev - day ?d_curr - day ?c_prev - amount ?c_curr - amount)
    :precondition (and 
      (at_city ?c) 
      (current_day ?d_prev) 
      (next_day ?d_prev ?d_curr)
      (visit_count ?c ?c_prev) 
      (next_amount ?c_prev ?c_curr)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (current_day ?d_prev)) 
      (current_day ?d_curr)
      (not (visit_count ?c ?c_prev)) 
      (visit_count ?c ?c_curr)
    )
  )

  ;; The agent takes a direct flight to a new city, spending that day at the destination.
  (:action fly
    :parameters (?from - city ?to - city ?d_prev - day ?d_curr - day ?c_prev - amount ?c_curr - amount)
    :precondition (and 
      (at_city ?from) 
      (can_fly ?from ?to) 
      (current_day ?d_prev) 
      (next_day ?d_prev ?d_curr)
      (visit_count ?to ?c_prev) 
      (next_amount ?c_prev ?c_curr)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (at_city ?from)) 
      (at_city ?to)
      (not (current_day ?d_prev)) 
      (current_day ?d_curr)
      (not (visit_count ?to ?c_prev)) 
      (visit_count ?to ?c_curr)
    )
  )

  ;; Meeting a friend in Florence can occur if the agent is in Florence during the specified day window.
  (:action meet_friend
    :parameters (?d - day)
    :precondition (and 
      (at_city florence) 
      (current_day ?d) 
      (is_friend_window ?d)
    )
    :effect (met_friend)
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem travel_problem)
  (:domain travel_domain)
  (:objects
    florence barcelona helsinki origin - city
    d0 d1 d2 d3 d4 d5 d6 d7 d8 d9 d10 d11 d12 d13 d14 - day
    n0 n1 n2 n3 n4 n5 n6 - amount
  )
  (:init
    ;; Starting state
    (at_city origin)
    (current_day d0)
    
    ;; Initial visit counts for all relevant cities
    (visit_count florence n0)
    (visit_count barcelona n0)
    (visit_count helsinki n0)
    
    ;; City connectivity (Direct flights only)
    (can_fly barcelona florence)
    (can_fly florence barcelona)
    (can_fly helsinki barcelona)
    (can_fly barcelona helsinki)
    
    ;; Temporal sequence
    (next_day d0 d1)
    (next_day d1 d2)
    (next_day d2 d3)
    (next_day d3 d4)
    (next_day d4 d5)
    (next_day d5 d6)
    (next_day d6 d7)
    (next_day d7 d8)
    (next_day d8 d9)
    (next_day d9 d10)
    (next_day d10 d11)
    (next_day d11 d12)
    (next_day d12 d13)
    (next_day d13 d14)
    
    ;; Counter sequence
    (next_amount n0 n1)
    (next_amount n1 n2)
    (next_amount n2 n3)
    (next_amount n3 n4)
    (next_amount n4 n5)
    (next_amount n5 n6)
    
    ;; Friend availability window (inclusive of day 9 to 14)
    (is_friend_window d9)
    (is_friend_window d10)
    (is_friend_window d11)
    (is_friend_window d12)
    (is_friend_window d13)
    (is_friend_window d14)
  )
  (:goal
    (and
      ;; Visit durations
      (visit_count florence n6)
      (visit_count barcelona n5)
      (visit_count helsinki n5)
      
      ;; Special condition
      (met_friend)
      
      ;; Final trip completion
      (current_day d14)
    )
  )
)
</problem>
================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain travel_domain)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types city day amount)

  (:predicates
    (at_city ?c - city)
    (current_day ?d - day)
    (visit_count ?c - city ?a - amount)
    (can_fly ?c1 - city ?c2 - city)
    (next_day ?d1 - day ?d2 - day)
    (next_amount ?a1 - amount ?a2 - amount)
    (is_friend_window ?d - day)
    (met_friend)
  )

  ;; The agent starts the trip by arriving at the first city on Day 1.
  (:action arrive
    :parameters (?to - city ?d_start - day ?d_first - day ?c_zero - amount ?c_one - amount)
    :precondition (and 
      (at_city origin) 
      (current_day ?d_start) 
      (next_day ?d_start ?d_first)
      (visit_count ?to ?c_zero) 
      (next_amount ?c_zero ?c_one)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (at_city origin)) 
      (at_city ?to)
      (not (current_day ?d_start)) 
      (current_day ?d_first)
      (not (visit_count ?to ?c_zero)) 
      (visit_count ?to ?c_one)
    )
  )

  ;; The agent spends a full day in their current city.
  (:action stay
    :parameters (?c - city ?d_prev - day ?d_curr - day ?c_prev - amount ?c_curr - amount)
    :precondition (and 
      (at_city ?c) 
      (current_day ?d_prev) 
      (next_day ?d_prev ?d_curr)
      (visit_count ?c ?c_prev) 
      (next_amount ?c_prev ?c_curr)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (current_day ?d_prev)) 
      (current_day ?d_curr)
      (not (visit_count ?c ?c_prev)) 
      (visit_count ?c ?c_curr)
    )
  )

  ;; The agent takes a direct flight to a new city, spending that day at the destination.
  (:action fly
    :parameters (?from - city ?to - city ?d_prev - day ?d_curr - day ?c_prev - amount ?c_curr - amount)
    :precondition (and 
      (at_city ?from) 
      (can_fly ?from ?to) 
      (current_day ?d_prev) 
      (next_day ?d_prev ?d_curr)
      (visit_count ?to ?c_prev) 
      (next_amount ?c_prev ?c_curr)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (at_city ?from)) 
      (at_city ?to)
      (not (current_day ?d_prev)) 
      (current_day ?d_curr)
      (not (visit_count ?to ?c_prev)) 
      (visit_count ?to ?c_curr)
    )
  )

  ;; Meeting a friend in Florence can occur if the agent is in Florence during the specified day window.
  (:action meet_friend
    :parameters (?d - day)
    :precondition (and 
      (at_city florence) 
      (current_day ?d) 
      (is_friend_window ?d)
    )
    :effect (met_friend)
  )
)

PROBLEM
------------------------------
(define (problem travel_problem)
  (:domain travel_domain)
  (:objects
    florence barcelona helsinki origin - city
    d0 d1 d2 d3 d4 d5 d6 d7 d8 d9 d10 d11 d12 d13 d14 - day
    n0 n1 n2 n3 n4 n5 n6 - amount
  )
  (:init
    ;; Starting state
    (at_city origin)
    (current_day d0)
    
    ;; Initial visit counts for all relevant cities
    (visit_count florence n0)
    (visit_count barcelona n0)
    (visit_count helsinki n0)
    
    ;; City connectivity (Direct flights only)
    (can_fly barcelona florence)
    (can_fly florence barcelona)
    (can_fly helsinki barcelona)
    (can_fly barcelona helsinki)
    
    ;; Temporal sequence
    (next_day d0 d1)
    (next_day d1 d2)
    (next_day d2 d3)
    (next_day d3 d4)
    (next_day d4 d5)
    (next_day d5 d6)
    (next_day d6 d7)
    (next_day d7 d8)
    (next_day d8 d9)
    (next_day d9 d10)
    (next_day d10 d11)
    (next_day d11 d12)
    (next_day d12 d13)
    (next_day d13 d14)
    
    ;; Counter sequence
    (next_amount n0 n1)
    (next_amount n1 n2)
    (next_amount n2 n3)
    (next_amount n3 n4)
    (next_amount n4 n5)
    (next_amount n5 n6)
    
    ;; Friend availability window (inclusive of day 9 to 14)
    (is_friend_window d9)
    (is_friend_window d10)
    (is_friend_window d11)
    (is_friend_window d12)
    (is_friend_window d13)
    (is_friend_window d14)
  )
  (:goal
    (and
      ;; Visit durations
      (visit_count florence n6)
      (visit_count barcelona n5)
      (visit_count helsinki n5)
      
      ;; Special condition
      (met_friend)
      
      ;; Final trip completion
      (current_day d14)
    )
  )
)

The day of the flight counts toward the duration of both \n  ;; the origin city (last day there) and the destination city (first day there).\n  (:action fly\n    :parameters (?from - city ?to - city ?d_prev - day ?d_curr - day ?v_f_old - count ?v_f_new - count ?v_t_old - count ?v_t_new - count)\n    :precondition (and \n      (trip_started)\n      (at_city ?from) \n      (can_fly ?from ?to) \n      (current_day ?d_prev) \n      (next_day ?d_prev ?d_curr) \n      (day_count ?from ?v_f_old) \n      (next_val ?v_f_old ?v_f_new) \n      (day_count ?to ?v_t_old) \n      (next_val ?v_t_old ?v_t_new)\n    )\n    :effect (and \n      (not (at_city ?from)) \n      (at_city ?to) \n      (not (current_day ?d_prev)) \n      (current_day ?d_curr) \n      (not (day_count ?from ?v_f_old)) \n      (day_count ?from ?v_f_new) \n      (not (day_count ?to ?v_t_old)) \n      (day_count ?to ?v_t_new)\n    )\n  )\n\n  ;; Meet a friend in Florence if the agent is there during the allowed window.\n  (:action meet_friend\n    :parameters (?d - day)\n    :precondition (and \n      (at_city florence) \n      (current_day ?d) \n      (is_friend_window ?d)\n    )\n    :effect (met_friend)\n  )\n)",
    "pddl_problem": "(define (problem trip_planning_example)\n  (:domain trip_planning)\n  (:objects\n    helsinki barcelona florence - city\n    d1 d2 d3 d4 d5 d6 d7 d8 d9 d10 d11 d12 d13 d14 - day\n    v0 v1 v2 v3 v4 v5 v6 - count\n  )\n  (:init\n    ;; Initial visit counts for all cities\n    (day_count helsinki v0)\n    (day_count barcelona v0)\n    (day_count florence v0)\n\n    ;; Temporal adjacency\n    (next_day d1 d2)\n    (next_day d2 d3)\n    (next_day d3 d4)\n    (next_day d4 d5)\n    (next_day d5 d6)\n    (next_day d6 d7)\n    (next_day d7 d8)\n    (next_day d8 d9)\n    (next_day d9 d10)\n    (next_day d10 d11)\n    (next_day d11 d12)\n    (next_day d12 d13)\n    (next_day d13 d14)\n\n    ;; Count sequence\n    (next_val v0 v1)\n    (next_val v1 v2)\n    (next_val v2 v3)\n    (next_val v3 v4)\n    (next_val v4 v5)\n    (next_val v5 v6)\n\n    ;; Flight connectivity\n    (can_fly helsinki barcelona)\n    (can_fly barcelona helsinki)\n    (can_fly barcelona florence)\n    (can_fly florence barcelona)\n\n    ;; Friend window in Florence\n    (is_friend_window d9)\n    (is_friend_window d10)\n    (is_friend_window d11)\n    (is_friend_window d12)\n    (is_friend_window d13)\n    (is_friend_window d14)\n  )\n  (:goal\n    (and\n      ;; Visit durations\n      (day_count florence v6)\n      (day_count barcelona v5)\n      (day_count helsinki v5)\n      \n      ;; Special meeting\n      (met_friend)\n      \n      ;; Trip completion within 14 days\n      (current_day d14)\n    )\n  )\n)",

*   **Helsinki:** 5 days (Day 1 to Day 5)

Riga and Manchester, from Manchester to Split.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 15 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain trip_planning)\n  (:requirements :strips :typing)\n  (:types city day num)\n  (:predicates\n    (at_city ?c - city ?d - day)\n    (visited ?c - city ?d - day)\n    (visit_count ?c - city ?n - num)\n    (next_day ?d1 ?d2 - day)\n    (next_num ?n1 ?n2 - num)\n    (has_flight ?c1 ?c2 - city)\n    (not_started)\n    (is_day1 ?d - day)\n    (is_num0 ?n - num)\n    (is_num1 ?n - num)\n  )\n\n  ;; Start the trip at any city on Day 1\n  (:action start_trip\n    :parameters (?c - city ?d1 - day ?n0 - num ?n1 - num)\n    :precondition (and (not_started) (is_day1 ?d1) (is_num0 ?n0) (is_num1 ?n1) (visit_count ?c ?n0))\n    :effect (and\n      (not (not_started))\n      (at_city ?c ?d1)\n      (visited ?c ?d1)\n      (not (visit_count ?c ?n0))\n      (visit_count ?c ?n1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  ;; Stay in the current city for one more day\n  (:action stay\n    :parameters (?c - city ?d_prev - day ?d_curr - day ?n_old - num ?n_new - num)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_city ?c ?d_prev)\n      (next_day ?d_prev ?d_curr)\n      (visit_count ?c ?n_old)\n      (next_num ?n_old ?n_new)\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_city ?c ?d_prev))\n      (at_city ?c ?d_curr)\n      (visited ?c ?d_curr)\n      (not (visit_count ?c ?n_old))\n      (visit_count ?c ?n_new)\n    )\n  )\n\n  ;; Fly from one city to another on a specific day.\n  ;; The travel day counts as a visit day for both the departure and arrival city.\n  (:action fly\n    :parameters (?c_from - city ?c_to - city ?d_prev - day ?d_curr - day ?n_from_old - num ?n_from_new - num ?n_to_old - num ?n_to_new - num)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_city ?c_from ?d_prev)\n      (next_day ?d_prev ?d_curr)\n      (has_flight ?c_from ?c_to)\n      (visit_count ?c_from ?n_from_old)\n      (next_num ?n_from_old ?n_from_new)\n      (visit_count ?c_to ?n_to_old)\n      (next_num ?n_to_old ?n_to_new)\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_city ?c_from ?d_prev))\n      (at_city ?c_to ?d_curr)\n      (visited ?c_from ?d_curr)\n      (visited ?c_to ?d_curr)\n      (not (visit_count ?c_from ?n_from_old))\n      (visit_count ?c_from ?n_from_new)\n      (not (visit_count ?c_to ?n_to_old))\n      (visit_count ?c_to ?n_to_new)\n    )\n  )\n)",

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_2
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL plan, here is your 11-day trip itinerary:

1.  **Day 1: Start Trip**
    Begin your journey in **Reykjavik**. This marks your first day in the city.

2.  **Day 2: Fly to Vienna**
    Depart from Reykjavik and take a direct flight to **Vienna**. Since the travel day counts as a visit to both locations, you complete your 2-day requirement for Reykjavik and begin your stay in Vienna.

3.  **Day 3: Stay in Vienna**
    Spend the entire day exploring **Vienna**.

4.  **Day 4: Stay in Vienna**
    Continue your stay in **Vienna**.

5.  **Day 5: Stay in Vienna**
    Continue your stay in **Vienna**.

6.  **Day 6: Stay in Vienna**
    Continue your stay in **Vienna**.

7.  **Day 7: Stay in Vienna**
    Spend another day in **Vienna**.

8.  **Day 8: Fly to Venice (Wedding Starts)**
    Take a direct flight from Vienna to **Venice**. This travel day counts as your 7th and final day in Vienna and your 1st day in Venice. You have arrived in time for the wedding, which takes place from Day 8 to Day 11.

9.  **Day 9: Stay in Venice**
    Spend the day in **Venice** attending the wedding.

10. **Day 10: Stay in Venice**
    Continue your stay in **Venice** for the wedding.

11. **Day 11: Final Day in Venice**
    Spend your final day in **Venice**. This completes your 4-day requirement for Venice and marks the end of your 11-day trip.

You want to meet a friend in Bucharest between day 5 and day 6. You would like to visit Berlin for 3 days. You would like to visit Warsaw for 3 days.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Berlin and Warsaw, Warsaw and Bucharest.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 6 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.

Based on the provided PDDL plan and specification, here is your 6-day trip itinerary:

**Day 1: Arrival**
*   **Location:** Berlin
*   Your trip begins in Berlin, where you spend your first day.

**Day 2: Berlin**
*   **Action:** Stay in Berlin.
*   Spend the entirety of Day 2 exploring Berlin, bringing your total stay in the city to 2 days.

**Day 3: Travel to Warsaw**
*   **Action:** Fly from Berlin to Warsaw.
*   Take a direct flight from Berlin to Warsaw. In this itinerary, the travel day counts as time spent in both the city of origin and the destination. By the end of today, you have completed your 3-day requirement for Berlin and have spent 1 day in Warsaw.

**Day 4: Warsaw**
*   **Action:** Stay in Warsaw.
*   Spend Day 4 in Warsaw, bringing your total stay in the city to 2 days.

**Day 5: Travel to Bucharest**
*   **Action:** Fly from Warsaw to Bucharest.
*   Take a direct flight from Warsaw to Bucharest. This travel day completes your 3-day requirement for Warsaw. Additionally, arriving in Bucharest today satisfies the first half of your commitment to meet your friend there (Day 5).

**Day 6: Bucharest**
*   **Action:** Stay in Bucharest.
*   Spend your final day in Bucharest. This completes your 2-day requirement for the city and allows you to meet your friend as planned between Day 5 and Day 6.

**Summary of Requirements Met:**
*   **Total Duration:** 6 Days.
*   **Berlin:** 3 Days (Day 1, Day 2, and Day 3 travel).
*   **Warsaw:** 3 Days (Day 3 travel, Day 4, and Day 5 travel).
*   **Bucharest:** 2 Days (Day 5 travel and Day 6).
*   **Friend Meeting:** Present in Bucharest on Day 5 and Day 6.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL plan, here is the sequence of actions for your 12-day trip through Munich, Seville, and Tallinn:

1.  **Day 1: Arrive in Munich**
    Begin your trip by arriving at your first city, Munich.

2.  **Day 1: Fly from Munich to Seville**
    Take a direct flight from Munich to Seville. This travel day counts as a visit to both Munich and Seville.

3.  **Day 2: Fly from Seville to Munich**
    Take a direct flight from Seville back to Munich.

4.  **Day 3: Fly from Munich to Seville**
    Take a direct flight from Munich to Seville.

5.  **Day 4: Fly from Seville to Munich**
    Take a direct flight from Seville back to Munich.

6.  **Day 5: Fly from Munich to Seville**
    Take a direct flight from Munich to Seville.

7.  **Day 6: Fly from Seville to Munich**
    Take a direct flight from Seville back to Munich.

8.  **Day 7: Fly from Munich to Seville**
    Take a direct flight from Munich to Seville. (This completes the necessary 7 days of visiting Seville).

9.  **Day 8: Fly from Seville to Munich**
    Take a direct flight from Seville back to Munich.

10. **Day 9: Fly from Munich to Seville**
    Take a direct flight from Munich to Seville.

11. **Day 10: Fly from Seville to Munich**
    Take a direct flight from Seville back to Munich. (This ensures you are in position for your next destination).

12. **Day 11: Fly from Munich to Tallinn**
    Take a direct flight from Munich to Tallinn. This marks the start of your 2-day visit to Tallinn and satisfies the requirement to be in the city to meet your friend.

13. **Day 12: Fly from Tallinn to Munich**
    Take a direct flight from Tallinn back to Munich. This completes your second day in Tallinn and concludes the 12-day travel schedule.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 16 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You plan to stay in Oslo for 6 days. You plan to stay in Porto for 7 days. You would like to visit Dubrovnik for 5 days. During day 12 and day 16, you have to attend a conference in Dubrovnik.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Oslo and Dubrovnik, Porto and Oslo.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 16 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.

*   **Residency:** Spend time in **Oslo** (Total: 5 days).
*   **Travel:** Take a direct flight from **Oslo** to **Dubrovnik**.
*   **Residency:** Record your 4th day of stay in **Dubrovnik**.

### **Day 16 (Conference Day)**
*   **Conference/Residency:** Attend the final day of the conference in **Dubrovnik** (Total: 5 days).
*   **Travel:** Take a direct flight from **Dubrovnik** back to **Oslo**.
*   **Residency:** Spend time in **Oslo** to complete your stay requirement (Total: 6 days).
*   **Travel:** Take a final direct flight from **Oslo** back to **Porto**.
*   **Residency:** Complete your stay in **Porto** (Total: 7 days).

**Trip Summary:**
- **Total Duration:** 16 Days
- **Porto Stay:** 7 Days
- **Oslo Stay:** 6 Days
- **Dubrovnik Stay:** 5 Days (including mandatory conference attendance on Day 12 and Day 16)

Munich and Dubrovnik, Berlin and Munich.

10. **Day 8:** Conclude your 8-day trip in **Dubrovnik**.

*   **Dubrovnik:** 3 days (Days 6, 7, 8).

from Dubrovnik to Istanbul, Istanbul and Venice.
